ELITE GROUP STAGE
-----------------

Participants:
- 11 PFL Path winners;
- 11 FNL Path winners;
- 11 RPL clubs that do not participate in Eurocups.

Byes:

Lokomotiv Moskva
Rubin Kazan
FC Sochi

Point system:
Win (W) - 3 points;
Win on penalties (WP) - 2 points;
Loss on penalties (LP) - 1 point;
Loss (L) - 0 points.

The group winners qualify for the Round of 16.
